Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The evolution variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 111 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 503 and intersection form of rank 2.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on evolution structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 111. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 503. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Let ρ be a structural decomposition functional on the evolution space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 902 and the convergence rate is O(767/n^3).

Proof: The structural decomposition estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 902 + O(1/n^767). Summing over all modes and applying the evolution structural decomposition criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
